What is its implementation, request/response format, and what APIs does it call?\\n\\nPerfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me provide a comprehensive answer about the `create_pair` endpoint.\\n\\n## The `create_pair` Endpoint\\n\\nThe `create_pair` endpoint is implemented in [server/app.py:731-1044](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L731-L1044) as an asynchronous POST handler that generates code completions from two different LLM models concurrently for A/B comparison.\\n\\n### Request Format\\n\\n**Required fields:**\\n- `prefix` (string) - Code before the cursor position\\n- `userId` (string) - User identifier\\n- `privacy` (enum) - Privacy setting: `\\\"Private\\\"`, `\\\"Debug\\\"`, or `\\\"Research\\\"`\\n\\n**Optional fields:**\\n- `pairId` (string) - Pair identifier (generated as UUID if not provided)\\n- `suffix` (string) - Code after the cursor position (default: `\\\"\\\"`)\\n- `temperature` (number) - LLM temperature parameter (default: `0.5`)\\n- `max_tokens` or `maxTokens` (number) - Maximum tokens to generate (default: `1024`)\\n- `top_p` or `topP` (number) - Top-p sampling parameter (default: `1`)\\n- `max_lines` or `maxLines` (number) - Maximum lines to generate (default: `5`)\\n- `modelTags` (string[]) - Tags to filter model selection (default: `[]`)\\n\\n### Response Format\\n\\n```typescript\\n{\\n  \\\"pairId\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n  \\\"completionItems\\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\\"completionId\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"userId\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"timestamp\\\": number,\\n      \\\"prompt\\\": string | object[],\\n      \\\"prefix\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"suffix\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"full_prefix\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"full_suffix\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"completion\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"raw_completion\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"model\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"pairCompletionId\\\": \\\"string\\\",\\n      \\\"pairIndex\\\": 0 | 1,\\n      \\\"latency\\\": number,\\n      \\\"model_1_cost\\\": number,  // (on first item only)\\n      \\\"model_2_cost\\\": number,  // (on second item only)\\n      \\\"prompt_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"prefix_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"suffix_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"completion_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"prompt_token_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"prefix_token_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"suffix_token_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"full_prefix_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"full_suffix_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"completion_token_length\\\": number,\\n      \\\"privacy\\\": \\\"Private\\\" | \\\"Debug\\\" | \\\"Research\\\",\\n      \\\"versionBackend\\\": \\\"string\\\"\\n    },\\n    // Second completion item with similar structure\\n  ]\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### Implementation Flow\\n\\n1. **Request Parsing** ([L738-778](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L738-L778)) - Validates required fields and parses privacy setting\\n\\n2. **Data Preparation** ([L780-810](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L780-L810)) - Extracts parameters, generates `pairId` if needed, creates `State` and `LLMOptions` objects\\n\\n3. **Model Selection** ([L813-815](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L813-L815)) - Calls `self.select_models(tags)` to pick two models for comparison\\n\\n4. **Token Truncation** ([L817-827](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L817-L827)) - Limits prefix/suffix to max input tokens using tiktoken encoding\\n\\n5. **Concurrent LLM Calls** ([L829-850](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L829-L850)) - Executes two `timed_create()` calls in parallel using `asyncio.gather()`\\n\\n6. **Response Preparation** ([L854-992](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L854-L992)) - Generates UUIDs, tokenizes all text, calculates costs, builds completion items\\n\\n7. **Background Tasks** ([L1000-1030](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L1000-L1030)) - Uploads to Firebase and tracks in Amplitude\\n\\n### External API Calls\\n\\nThe endpoint makes the following external API calls:\\n\\n**1. LLM Provider APIs** (via `timed_create()` in [L86-109](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L86-L109))\\n   - Calls `client.create()` on two different model clients concurrently\\n   - Supported providers (from [server/apis/](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/tree/server/apis)):\\n     - OpenAI (`openai_client.py`)\\n     - Anthropic (`anthropic_client.py`)\\n     - Google Gemini (`gemini_client.py`)\\n     - Mistral (`mistral_client.py`)\\n     - Fireworks (`fireworks_client.py`)\\n     - DeepSeek (`deepseek_client.py`, `deepseek_fim_client.py`)\\n     - Mercury Coder (`mercury_coder.py`)\\n\\n**2. Amplitude Analytics** (background tasks)\\n   - `create_pair` event - Tracked immediately ([L792-795](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L792-L795))\\n   - `create_pair_success` event - Tracked with model metadata and costs ([L1002-1017](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L1002-L1017))\\n\\n**3. Firebase Firestore** (background tasks)\\n   - Uploads both completion items to the `all_completions` collection ([L1018-1030](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L1018-L1030))\\n\\n### Rate Limiting\\n\\nThe endpoint has a rate limit of **300 requests per minute** ([L732](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L732)).\\n\\n## Sources\\n\\n- Let me provide a comprehensive answer about the `create_pair` endpoint.\n\n## The `create_pair` Endpoint\n\nThe `create_pair` endpoint is implemented in [server/app.py:731-1044](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L731-L1044) as an asynchronous POST handler that generates code completions from two different LLM models concurrently for A/B comparison.\n\n### Request Format\n\n**Required fields:**\n- `prefix` (string) - Code before the cursor position\n- `userId` (string) - User identifier\n- `privacy` (enum) - Privacy setting: `\"Private\"`, `\"Debug\"`, or `\"Research\"`\n\n**Optional fields:**\n- `pairId` (string) - Pair identifier (generated as UUID if not provided)\n- `suffix` (string) - Code after the cursor position (default: `\"\"`)\n- `temperature` (number) - LLM temperature parameter (default: `0.5`)\n- `max_tokens` or `maxTokens` (number) - Maximum tokens to generate (default: `1024`)\n- `top_p` or `topP` (number) - Top-p sampling parameter (default: `1`)\n- `max_lines` or `maxLines` (number) - Maximum lines to generate (default: `5`)\n- `modelTags` (string[]) - Tags to filter model selection (default: `[]`)\n\n### Response Format\n\n```typescript\n{\n  \"pairId\": \"string\",\n  \"completionItems\": [\n    {\n      \"completionId\": \"string\",\n      \"userId\": \"string\",\n      \"timestamp\": number,\n      \"prompt\": string | object[],\n      \"prefix\": \"string\",\n      \"suffix\": \"string\",\n      \"full_prefix\": \"string\",\n      \"full_suffix\": \"string\",\n      \"completion\": \"string\",\n      \"raw_completion\": \"string\",\n      \"model\": \"string\",\n      \"pairCompletionId\": \"string\",\n      \"pairIndex\": 0 | 1,\n      \"latency\": number,\n      \"model_1_cost\": number,  // (on first item only)\n      \"model_2_cost\": number,  // (on second item only)\n      \"prompt_length\": number,\n      \"prefix_length\": number,\n      \"suffix_length\": number,\n      \"completion_length\": number,\n      \"prompt_token_length\": number,\n      \"prefix_token_length\": number,\n      \"suffix_token_length\": number,\n      \"full_prefix_length\": number,\n      \"full_suffix_length\": number,\n      \"completion_token_length\": number,\n      \"privacy\": \"Private\" | \"Debug\" | \"Research\",\n      \"versionBackend\": \"string\"\n    },\n    // Second completion item with similar structure\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### Implementation Flow\n\n1. **Request Parsing** ([L738-778](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L738-L778)) - Validates required fields and parses privacy setting\n\n2. **Data Preparation** ([L780-810](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L780-L810)) - Extracts parameters, generates `pairId` if needed, creates `State` and `LLMOptions` objects\n\n3. **Model Selection** ([L813-815](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L813-L815)) - Calls `self.select_models(tags)` to pick two models for comparison\n\n4. **Token Truncation** ([L817-827](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L817-L827)) - Limits prefix/suffix to max input tokens using tiktoken encoding\n\n5. **Concurrent LLM Calls** ([L829-850](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L829-L850)) - Executes two `timed_create()` calls in parallel using `asyncio.gather()`\n\n6. **Response Preparation** ([L854-992](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L854-L992)) - Generates UUIDs, tokenizes all text, calculates costs, builds completion items\n\n7. **Background Tasks** ([L1000-1030](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L1000-L1030)) - Uploads to Firebase and tracks in Amplitude\n\n### External API Calls\n\nThe endpoint makes the following external API calls:\n\n**1. LLM Provider APIs** (via `timed_create()` in [L86-109](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L86-L109))\n   - Calls `client.create()` on two different model clients concurrently\n   - Supported providers (from [server/apis/](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/tree/server/apis)):\n     - OpenAI (`openai_client.py`)\n     - Anthropic (`anthropic_client.py`)\n     - Google Gemini (`gemini_client.py`)\n     - Mistral (`mistral_client.py`)\n     - Fireworks (`fireworks_client.py`)\n     - DeepSeek (`deepseek_client.py`, `deepseek_fim_client.py`)\n     - Mercury Coder (`mercury_coder.py`)\n\n**2. Amplitude Analytics** (background tasks)\n   - `create_pair` event - Tracked immediately ([L792-795](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L792-L795))\n   - `create_pair_success` event - Tracked with model metadata and costs ([L1002-1017](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L1002-L1017))\n\n**3. Firebase Firestore** (background tasks)\n   - Uploads both completion items to the `all_completions` collection ([L1018-1030](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L1018-L1030))\n\n### Rate Limiting\n\nThe endpoint has a rate limit of **300 requests per minute** ([L732](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/copilot-arena--latest/-/blob/server/app.py?L732)).\n\n## Sources\n\n- total_prob = sum(probabilities)\n   382→        if total_prob == 0:\n   383→            raise HTTPException(\n   384→                status_code=500, detail=\"Distribution probabilities sum to 0\"\n   385→            )\n   386→        normalized_probs = [p / total_prob for p in probabilities]\n   387→\n   388→        # Select a pair based on the normalized distribution\n   389→        selected_pair = valid_pairs[\n   390→            np.random.choice(len(valid_pairs), p=normalized_probs)\n   391→        ]\n   392→\n   393→        # Randomly shuffle the order\n   394→        if np.random.random() < 0.5:\n   395→            selected_pair = selected_pair[::-1]\n   396→\n   397→        # Get the clients for the selected models\n   398→        client1: IBaseClient = self.model_clients.get(selected_pair[0])\n   399→        client2: IBaseClient = self.model_clients.get(selected_pair[1])\n   400→\n   401→        return list(selected_pair), client1, client2\n Determine if this is an edit request\n        is_edit = \"edit\" in tags\n        dist_key = \"edit\" if is_edit else \"autocomplete\"\n\n        # Get available models with required tags\n        tagged_models = get_models_by_tags(tags, self.models, self.tag_to_models)\n        if len(tagged_models) == 0:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f\"Error in tagged models\")\n\n        # Get all valid model pairs and their probabilities from the distribution\n        valid_pairs = []\n        probabilities = []\n\n        for pair_str, prob in self.distributions[dist_key].items():\n            model1, model2 = pair_str.split(\"|||\")\n            if model1 in tagged_models and model2 in tagged_models:\n                valid_pairs.append((model1, model2))\n                probabilities.append(prob)\n\n        if not valid_pairs:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=500, detail=f\"No valid model pairs found for tags: {tags}\"\n            )\n\n        # Normalize probabilities\n        total_prob = sum(probabilities)\n        if total_prob == 0:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=500, detail=\"Distribution probabilities sum to 0\"\n            )\n        normalized_probs = [p / total_prob for p in probabilities]\n\n        # Select a pair based on the normalized distribution\n        selected_pair = valid_pairs[\n            np.random.choice(len(valid_pairs), p=normalized_probs)\n        ]\n\n        # Randomly shuffle the order\n        if np.random.random() < 0.5:\n            selected_pair = selected_pair[::-1]\n\n        # Took {latency:.4f} seconds\"\n        )\n        print(f\"result {model}: {result.text}\")\n        MODEL_CREATE_COUNT.labels(\n            client=client.__class__.__name__, model=model, status=\"success\"\n        ).inc()\n        MODEL_CREATE_LATENCY.labels(\n            client=client.__class__.__name__, model=model\n        ).observe(latency)\n        return result, latency\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error caught in timed_create by {model}: {e}\")\n        MODEL_CREATE_COUNT.labels(\n            client=client.__class__.__name__, model=model, status=\"error\"\n        ).inc()\n        raise e\n\n\n# Used exclusively for load testing\nasync def timed_create_test(client, state, model, options):\n    print(f\"MOCK: Starting {client.__class__.__name__}.create for model {model}\")\n    start_time = asyncio.get_event_loop().time()\n    # Wait for 500ms to 1500 ms\n    result = await asyncio.sleep(np.random.uniform(0.5, 1.5))\n    result = \"test\"\n    end_time = asyncio.get_event_loop().time()\n    latency = end_time - start_time\n    print(\n        f\"MOCK: Finished {client.__class__.__name__}.create for model {model}. Get user_id and model from request if available\n            user_id = None\n            model = None\n            try:\n                body = await request.json()\n                user_id = body.get(\"userId\")\n                model = body.get(\"model\")\n            except:\n                pass\n\n            try:\n                return await func(*args, **kwargs)\n            except HTTPException:\n                raise\n            except Exception as e:\n                if isinstance(e, ModelTimeoutError):\n                    error_msg = f\"Service temporarily unavailable due to timeout for model: {e.model}\"\n                    logger.error(error_msg)\n                    ERROR_COUNT.labels(\n                        method=method,\n                        endpoint=endpoint,\n                        exception=\"TimeoutError\",\n                        status=504,\n                    ).inc()\n\n                    # Track timeout error in Amplitude\n                    app_instance.amplitude.track(\n                        BaseEvent(\n                            event_type=\"error\",\n                            user_id=user_id or \"anonymous\",\n                            event_properties={\n                                \"error_type\": \"TimeoutError\",\n                                \"model\": e.model or model,\n                                \"endpoint\": endpoint,\n                                \"method\": method,\n                                \"status\": 504,\n                                \"error_message\": str(e),\n                            },\n                        )\n                    )\n\n                    raise HTTPException(\n                        status_code=504,\n                        detail=\"Timeout\",\n                    )\n                elif \"429\" in str(e):\n                    logger.error(\"Rate limit exceeded.\")\n                    ERROR_COUNT.labels(\n                        method=method,\n                        endpoint=endpoint,\n                        exception=\"RateLimitExceeded\",\n                        status=429,\n                    ).inc()\n\n                    # Track rate limit error in Amplitude\n                    app_instance.amplitude.track(\n                        BaseEvent(\n                            event_type=\"error\",\n                            user_id=user_id or \"anonymous\",\n                            event_properties={\n                                \"error_type\": \"RateLimitExceeded\",\n                                \"model\": model,\n                                \"endpoint\": endpoint,\n                                \"method\": method,\n                                \"status\": 429,\n                                \"error_message\": str(e),\n                            },\n                        )\n                    )\n\n                    raise HTTPException(\n                        status_code=429,\n                        detail=\"Rate limit exceeded. Please try again later.\",\n                    )\n                else:\n                    error_msg = f\"An error occurred: {str(e)}\\n\\nTraceback:\\n{traceback.format_exc()}\"\n                    logger.error(error_msg)\n                    ERROR_COUNT.labels(\n                        method=method,\n                        endpoint=endpoint,\n                        exception=\"ServerError\",\n                        status=500,\n                    ).inc()\n\n                    # Track general server error in Amplitude\n                    app_instance.amplitude.track(\n                        BaseEvent(\n                            event_type=\"error\",\n                            user_id=user_id or \"anonymous\",\n                            event_properties={\n                                \"error_type\": \"ServerError\",\n                                \"model\": model,\n                                \"endpoint\": endpoint,\n                                \"method\": method,\n                                \"status\": 500,\n                                \"error_message\": str(e),\n                                \"traceback\": traceback.format_exc(),\n                            },\n                        )\n                    )\n\n                    raise HTTPException(\n                        status_code=500, detail=\"An internal server error occurred.\"\n                    )\n\n        return wrapper\n\n    return decorator\n\n\nclass FastAPIApp:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.FIREBASE_COLLECTIONS_KEY = \"firebase_collections\"\n        self.amplitude = Amplitude(AMPLITUDE_API_KEY)\n        self.settings = get_settings()\n        self.version_backend = self.settings.get(\"version_backend\")\n        self.limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)\n        self.app = FastAPI()\n        self.app.state.limiter = self.limiter\n        self.app.add_exception_handler(RateLimitExceeded, _rate_limit_exceeded_handler)\n        self.firebase_client = FirebaseClient()\n        # Set up user repository (Must come after firebase client for auth)\n        self.user_repo = UserRepository()\n\n        # Set up all clients\n        self.model_clients = {}\n        self.tag_to_models = {}\n        self.add_client(OpenAIClient())\n        self.add_client(AnthropicClient())\n        self.add_client(GeminiClient())\n        # self.add_client(FireworksClient())\n        # self.add_client(DeepseekClient())\n        # FiM Clients\n        self.add_client(MistralClient())\n        self.add_client(DeepseekFimClient())\n        self.encoding = tiktoken.encoding_for_model(\"gpt-4\")\n\n        logger.info(f\"models: {self.models}\")\n\n        self.user_scores_cache = {}\n\n        # Initialize distributions for each request type\n        self.distributions = {\n            \"autocomplete\": self.settings.get(\"dist\", {}).get(\"autocomplete\", {}),\n            \"edit\": self.settings.get(\"dist\", {}).get(\"edit\", {}),\n        }\n        print(self.distributions)\n\n        self.gcp_client = GCPClient()\n\n        # Initialize global_outcomes_df\n        self.update_global_outcomes_df()\n\n        # Set up scheduler for updating global_outcomes_df\n        self.scheduler = AsyncIOScheduler()\n        self.scheduler.add_job(\n            self.update_global_outcomes_df,\n            trigger=IntervalTrigger(hours=24),\n            id=\"update_global_outcomes_df\",\n            name=\"Update global outcomes DataFrame every day\",\n            replace_existing=True,\n        )\n        self.scheduler.start()\n\n        self.setup_routes()\n        logger.info(\"API is starting up\")\n\n    def update_global_outcomes_df(self):\n        try:\n            bucket_name = self.settings.get(\"bucket_name\")\n            file_path = self.settings.get(\"outcomes_file_path\")\n\n            self.global_outcomes_df = self.gcp_client.get_outcomes_df(\n                bucket_name=bucket_name, file_path=file_path\n            )\n            logger.info(self.global_outcomes_df)\n            logger.info(\"Updated global_outcomes_df from GCS\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(f\"Failed to update global_outcomes_df: {str(e)}\")\n            # Initialize empty DataFrame if fetch fails\n            self.global_outcomes_df = pd.DataFrame()\n\n    def add_client(self, client: IBaseClient):\n        for model in client.models:\n            if model in self.settings[\"models\"]:\n                self.model_clients[model] = client\n                for tag in self.settings[\"models\"][model][\"tags\"]:\n                    if tag not in self.tag_to_models:\n                        self.tag_to_models[tag] = set()\n                    self.tag_to_models[tag].add(model)\n\n        self.models = list(self.model_clients.keys())\n\n    def select_models(self, tags):\n        # Determine if this is an edit request\n        is_edit = \"edit\" in tags\n        dist_key = \"edit\" if is_edit else \"autocomplete\"\n\n        # Get available models with required tags\n        tagged_models = get_models_by_tags(tags, self.models, self.tag_to_models)\n        if len(tagged_models) == 0:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f\"Error in tagged models\")\n\n        # Get all valid model pairs and their probabilities from the distribution\n        valid_pairs = []\n        probabilities = []\n\n        for pair_str, prob in self.distributions[dist_key].items():\n            model1, model2 = pair_str.split(\"|||\")\n            if model1 in tagged_models and model2 in tagged_models:\n                valid_pairs.append((model1, model2))\n                probabilities.append(prob)\n\n        if not valid_pairs:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=500, detail=f\"No valid model pairs found for tags: {tags}\"\n            )\n\n        # Normalize probabilities\n        total_prob = sum(probabilities)\n        if total_prob == 0:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=500, detail=\"Distribution probabilities sum to 0\"\n            )\n        normalized_probs = [p / total_prob for p in probabilities]\n\n        # Select a pair based on the normalized distribution\n        selected_pair = valid_pairs[\n            np.random.choice(len(valid_pairs), p=normalized_probs)\n        ]\n\n        # Randomly shuffle the order\n        if np.random.random() < 0.5:\n            selected_pair = selected_pair[::-1]\n\n        # Get the clients for the selected models\n        client1: IBaseClient = self.model_clients.get(selected_pair[0])\n        client2: IBaseClient = self.model_clients.get(selected_pair[1])\n\n        return list(selected_pair), client1, client2\n\n    def setup_routes(self):\n        @self.app.post(\"/create_edit_pair\")\n        @self.limiter.limit(\"300/minute\")\n        @handle_exceptions(self)\n        async def create_edit_pair(request: Request, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks):\n            start_time = time.time()\n            latency_breakdown = {}\n\n            # Convert byte string to base64-encoded string\n            user_data[self.PASSWORD_HASH_KEY] = base64.b64encode(password_hash).decode(\n                \"utf-8\"\n            )\n        if metadata is not None:\n            user_data[self.METADATA_KEY] = metadata\n        self.collection_ref.add(user_data)\n\n    def update_user(\n        self,\n        username: str = None,\n        old_password: str = None,\n        new_password: str = None,\n        metadata: dict = None,\n    ):\n        self.track_function_call(\"update_user\")\n        user_doc = self.collection_ref.where(self.USERNAME_KEY, \"==\", username).get()\n        if not user_doc:\n            raise UserNotFoundError(f\"User with username {username} not found\")\n        doc_id = user_doc[0].id\n        update_data = {}\n        self.authenticate(username, old_password)\n        if new_password:\n            password_hash = bcrypt.hashpw(\n                new_password.encode(\"utf-8\"), bcrypt.gensalt()\n            )\n            # Convert byte string to base64-encoded string\n            update_data[self.PASSWORD_HASH_KEY] = base64.b64encode(\n                password_hash\n            ).decode(\"utf-8\")\n        if metadata:\n            update_data[self.METADATA_KEY] = metadata\n        self.collection_ref.document(doc_id).update(update_data)\n\n    def delete_user(self, user_id: str):\n        self.track_function_call(\"delete_user\")\n        doc_ref = self.collection_ref.document(user_id)\n        if not doc_ref.get().exists:\n            raise UserNotFoundError(f\"User with ID {user_id} not found\")\n        doc_ref.delete()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":4,"newStart":1,"newLines":5,"lines":[" import base64","+import os"," from firebase_admin import firestore"," import bcrypt"," "]},{"oldStart":24,"oldLines":9,"newStart":25,"newLines":9,"lines":["     METADATA_KEY = \"metadata\""," ","     def __init__(self):","-        self.db = firestore.client()","-        collection_name = \"users\"","-        self.collection_ref = self.db.collection(collection_name)","+        self.mock_mode = os.getenv(\"MOCK_FIREBASE\", \"false\").lower() == \"true\"","+        self.db = None","+        self.collection_ref = None","         self.function_call_count = {","             \"get_user_by_id\": 0,","             \"get_user_by_username\": 0,"]},{"oldStart":36,"oldLines":6,"newStart":37,"newLines":14,"lines":["             \"delete_user\": 0,","         }"," ","+        if self.mock_mode:","+            print(\"UserRepository running in MOCK mode - no real Firebase operations\")","+            return","+","+        self.db = firestore.client()","+        collection_name = \"users\"","+        self.collection_ref = self.db.collection(collection_name)","+","     def track_function_call(self, function_name):","         self.function_call_count[function_name] += 1","         print(self.function_call_count)"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
